# hepar2.dice, translated by tddlog's scripts/dice2alea.py.
module AleaProgram
using Alea
# Julia would spend more time optimizing the long straight-line functions
# below than running them takes: they are interpreted, and Alea's own
# methods compile as usual.
Base.Experimental.@compiler_options compile=min optimize=0 infer=false

const EV = Ref{AnyBool}(true)
observe!(path, c) = (EV[] = EV[] & (!path | c); true)

function shr(x::DistUInt{W}, n::Integer) where W
    k = min(n, W)
    DistUInt{W}(vcat(fill(false, k), x.bits[1:W - k]))
end

components(x::Tuple) = reduce(vcat, map(components, x); init = Any[])
components(x) = Any[x]
width(::DistUInt{W}) where W = W

function warmup()
    a = flip(0.7)
    x = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    y = uniform(DistUInt{3}, 1, 6)
    z = ifelse(a, x + DistUInt{2}(1), x - DistUInt{2}(1))
    t = ifelse(flip(0.4), (a, z), (!a, x * x))
    e = prob_equals(z, DistUInt{2}(2)) | (x < z) | xor(a, flip(0.2))
    pr(flip(0.7))
    pr(a, z, y, t...; evidence = e)
    nothing
end

function chunk_6(x1888_bilirubin, x1909_itching)
    if x1909_itching !== false

     if x1911_itching !== true

     end

    end
    x1918_itching = x1909_itching === true ? x1910_itching : x1909_itching === false ? x1917_itching : ifelse(x1909_itching, x1910_itching, x1917_itching)
    return nothing
end

function dice_main()
    (x6_gallstones, x13_RHepatitis, x23_THepatitis, x129_ChHepatitis, x268_fibrosis, x290_Hyperbilirubinem, x312_PBC, x382_Steatosis, x383_cholesterol,) = chunk_1()
    x773_hepatomegaly = chunk_2(x13_RHepatitis, x23_THepatitis, x129_ChHepatitis, x290_Hyperbilirubinem, x312_PBC, x382_Steatosis, x383_cholesterol)
    (x846_Cirrhosis, x1146_alt,) = chunk_3(x13_RHepatitis, x23_THepatitis, x129_ChHepatitis, x268_fibrosis, x290_Hyperbilirubinem, x312_PBC, x382_Steatosis, x773_hepatomegaly)
    (x1500_inr, x1516_platelet, x1517_bleeding,) = chunk_4(x13_RHepatitis, x23_THepatitis, x129_ChHepatitis, x290_Hyperbilirubinem, x312_PBC, x382_Steatosis, x846_Cirrhosis, x1146_alt)
    (x1550_bleeding, x1888_bilirubin, x1909_itching,) = chunk_5(x6_gallstones, x13_RHepatitis, x23_THepatitis, x129_ChHepatitis, x290_Hyperbilirubinem, x312_PBC, x846_Cirrhosis, x1500_inr, x1516_platelet, x1517_bleeding)
    chunk_6(x1888_bilirubin, x1909_itching)
    return x1550_bleeding
end

function report(k, x, d)
    if x isa AnyBool
        println("alea ", k, " true ", d[true])
        println("alea ", k, " false ", d[false])
    else
        for v in 0:(2^width(x) - 1)
            println("alea ", k, " ", v, " ", d[v])
        end
    end
end

function run_program()
    EV[] = true
    stats = @timed begin
        comps = components(dice_main())
        dists = pr(comps...; evidence = EV[])
        (comps, length(comps) == 1 ? [dists] : dists)
    end
    comps, dists = stats.value
    for k in eachindex(comps)
        report(k, comps[k], dists[k])
    end
    println("alea_seconds ", stats.time)
    println("alea_compile_seconds ", get(stats, :compile_time, NaN))
end

warmup()
task = Task(run_program, 1 << 30)
schedule(task)
wait(task)
end
